- **short-play-writer**: 专业短剧/AI漫剧剧本创作助手，支持男频/女频爆款短剧、AI漫剧剧本撰写，包含最新行业成功要素、标准结构模板、爆款爽点设计和漫剧专属分镜规范。 ([canquedehuiyi-cpu/xiongbao-nenglishichang](https://github.com/canquedehuiyi-cpu/xiongbao-nenglishichang/tree/HEAD/skills/market/Knowledge/short-play-writer/short-play-writer))
- **short-post-creator**: Создатель вирусных коротких постов (до 1000 знаков): превращает исходный материал в 3-7 коротких постов. Использует референс-примеры для точного попадания в стиль, ритм и подачу. Триггеры: короткий пост, короткий текст для соцсетей. ([yasikvlad/content-system](https://github.com/yasikvlad/content-system/tree/HEAD/skills/short-post-creator) · ★7 · 3 copies)
- **short-prayer**: Draft a short, direct scriptural prayer (one paragraph) based on a passage. Use when the user runs /short-prayer or requests this BibleMate workflow. ([eliranwong/biblemate-agentic-workspace](https://github.com/eliranwong/biblemate-agentic-workspace/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/short-prayer) · ★13 · 4 copies)
- **short-prayer**: Draft a short, direct scriptural prayer (one paragraph) based on a passage. ([eliranwong/biblemate-agentic-workspace](https://github.com/eliranwong/biblemate-agentic-workspace/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/short-prayer) · ★13)
- **short-prompt**: Rules for a short prompt to an agent that already has the session context — the next turn of a Claude Code session, typed in the Ctrl+G buffer (a `.prompt` file in Zed). Use when writing, rewriting, or checking such a prompt. ([popoffvg/dotfiles](https://github.com/popoffvg/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/harness/skills/everyday/short-prompt) · ★2)
- **short-public-trial-face-slap**: Use when generating or analyzing short public trial, public face-slap, evidence reveal, reputation reversal, judgment scene, or staged confrontation outlines and chapter outlines. ([Smith-106/niko-buddy](https://github.com/Smith-106/niko-buddy/tree/HEAD/skills/SkillHub/TicaiSkill/short-public-trial-face-slap))
- **short-publish**: Post a finished vertical lesson from ~/Movies/lpm-lessons/tiktok/&lt;slug&gt;/ to YouTube Shorts (LPM channel, through Chrome) and TikTok (@lpm06557, from the user's iPhone through iPhone Mirroring) in parallel: the MP4, the caption and hashtags from post.txt, the headline cover, each platform's settings, then both links logged and the title listed in SHORTS.md. Use when the user asks to upload, publish or post a short, vertical, TikTok or YouTube Shorts video, to one platform or both. ([gug007/lpm](https://github.com/gug007/lpm/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/short-publish) · ★144)
- **short-publish**: End-to-end workflow for turning a local video into transcripts, burned subtitles, and scheduled multi-network posts via PostFlow CLI. Use when given a video path and publication date/time to transcribe, create copy for LinkedIn/X/IG/YouTube, upload the subtitled MP4, and schedule the content with `postflow`. ([antoniolg/agent-kit](https://github.com/antoniolg/agent-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/short-publish) · ★100)
- **short-publish**: Publishes {{PERSONA_NAME}} short-form videos to all 5 social platforms (Instagram Reels, TikTok, YouTube Shorts, Facebook, Twitter/X) via the Late API. ([kevinbadi/creator-os-starter](https://github.com/kevinbadi/creator-os-starter/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/short-publish) · ★12)
- **short-publish-folder**: **Publish your own short-form videos to all platforms from a local folder.** ([kevinbadi/creator-os-starter](https://github.com/kevinbadi/creator-os-starter/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/short-publish-folder) · ★12)
- **short-punchy-replies**: 1-3 sentence high-speed response discipline for CLI/mobile. ([Roshrak/tonelico](https://github.com/Roshrak/tonelico/tree/HEAD/agents/hermes/skills/communication/short-punchy-replies))
- **short-race**: Make a short vertical (9:16) video that races two AI models on the same prompt in lpm, one above the other: Run in duplicates puts each model in its own copy of the project, shown as two rows, the same prompt sent to both, the real finish times on the pane headers, then both results live in lpm's browser. Default prompt: a giraffe flying a one-seat plane as animated HTML. Models in, 1080x1920 MP4 + cover + post caption out. Use when the user asks for a TikTok, Reel or Short that compares models, such as "opus 5.5 max vs gpt 6 astra ultra", "opus 5.5 vs grok 4.7" (Claude models run in Claude C… ([gug007/lpm](https://github.com/gug007/lpm/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/short-race) · ★144)
- **short-range-chromatin-loop-verification**: Use when a ChIA-PET dataset (e.g. GM12878 CTCF paired-end FASTQ) must be analyzed for very short-range (&lt;8 kb) chromatin loops that standard pipelines miss due to self-ligation span cutoffs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/short-range-chromatin-loop-verification))
- **short-read-adapter-glocal-alignment**: Use when long-read FASTQ reads from a split hybrid LR-SR scRNA-seq library must have their 10x barcode-adjacent segment located and extracted, via adapter alignment with edit-distance scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-adapter-glocal-alignment))
- **short-read-adapter-trimming**: Use when raw FASTQ reads downloaded from SRA (e.g. accession SRR961514) are to be assembled with a de Bruijn graph assembler such as MetaCortex and adapter contamination must be removed first. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-adapter-trimming))
- **short-read-alignment-benchmarking**: Use when the goal is to quantify how an alignment-free scaffolding-graph builder compares with Bowtie/Bowtie2/Bwa/Bwa-Mem on short-read data, via precision/recall of link recovery and wall-clock runtime per k-mer size. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-alignment-benchmarking))
- **short-read-alignment-of-cdna-inserts**: Use when raw FASTQ files from droplet scRNA-seq (e.g. SRA SRP073767, 2x75 paired-end NextSeq500 output) must be converted into per-cell gene counts and Read1 must first be placed on a reference genome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/short-read-alignment-of-cdna-inserts))
- **short-read-alignment-parsing**: Use when WGS FASTQ reads must be aligned to a reference set of known MLST allele sequences and converted into a per-read, per-allele mapping-score table to feed downstream abundance-estimation ILP solvers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-alignment-parsing))
- **short-read-alignment-to-barcode-reference**: Use when barcode-bearing reads have been extracted and trimmed and must be assigned to a known synthetic barcode reference library (e.g. SPLINTR barcodes in FASTA) to produce per-barcode alignment counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/short-read-alignment-to-barcode-reference))
- **short-read-alignment-to-host-reference**: Use when microbial short- or long-read (meta)genomic FASTQ (single or paired, optionally gzip-compressed) is contaminated with human host sequences that must be removed for ethical and legal reasons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-alignment-to-host-reference))
- **short-read-alignment-to-reference**: Use when you need to map Illumina paired-end reads to a reference genome and recover split-read signal to support structural variation prediction across many strains. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-alignment-to-reference))
- **short-read-alignment-to-reference-catalog**: Use when you need to quantify cross-mapping between a query sequence set (e.g., fungal marker genes) and a reference catalog, or validate taxonomic database specificity via simulated read mapping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-alignment-to-reference-catalog))
- **short-read-alignment-to-reference-genome**: Use when raw FASTQ/FASTA reads from WGS or WES samples must be aligned to a reference genome (e.g., hg38) to prepare sorted, duplicate-marked, indexed BAM files for variant calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-alignment-to-reference-genome))
- **short-read-alignment-to-reference-genome**: Use when raw 10x Chromium paired-end FASTQ reads must be mapped to hg38 as a prerequisite for barcode-aware duplicate removal, fragment inference, and read coverage (CR) subsampling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-alignment-to-reference-genome--a754b7dce3b486d9))
- **short-read-alignment-to-reference-genome**: Use when you have reference-free variant calls (KisSNP2-format fasta / .snp file from ebwt2InDel) and an available reference genome, and you need reference-coordinate .vcf entries for downstream validation or comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-alignment-to-reference-genome--17580dcd143a76d3))
- **short-read-alignment-to-scaffolds**: Use when a draft scaffold FASTA contains N-run wildcards between contigs and paired-end short-read data is available to locally reassemble and fill scaffold gap regions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-alignment-to-scaffolds))
- **short-read-alignment-with-bowtie2**: Use when raw sequencing reads were simulated by software other than CAMISIM (e.g., ART) and must be placed against a reference genome (.fas) as input to STEMSIM's reads mode. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-alignment-with-bowtie2))
- **short-read-alignment-with-minimap2**: Use when short sequencing reads (FASTQ, e.g. single-end HiSeq or ONT-derived reads) must be aligned to one known viral reference genome (FASTA) as the step preceding consensus sequence reconstruction or variant analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-alignment-with-minimap2))
- **short-read-and-long-read-genome-mapping**: Use when a draft de novo assembly (FASTA/contigs) needs quality assessment at single-nucleotide resolution, and matched short-read (Illumina) plus long-read (PacBio HiFi, PacBio CLR, or ONT) sequencing data are available to remap onto it. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-and-long-read-genome-mapping))
- **short-read-and-long-read-mapping**: Use when you need to partition bacterial long- and short-read FASTQs against a draft assembly so only chromosomal reads are discarded and plasmid-derived reads (mapped to non-chromosome contigs or unmapped) are retained for downstream assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-and-long-read-mapping))
- **short-read-assembly**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia/tree/HEAD/Skills/Genomics/genome-assembly/short-read-assembly) · ★7)
- **short-read-assembly**: Assemble bacterial genomes from short-read sequencing data (Illumina). This skill focuses on producing the most contiguous draft possible from paired-end reads, utilizing De Bruijn graph assemblers and integrating quality control to ensure data integrity before assembly. Builds on the upstream read-qc-trimming skill. Has explicit ask-user stop points (SP8, SP9) that fire only when evidence is ambiguous. ([cheahhl814/bacterial-genome-analysis](https://github.com/cheahhl814/bacterial-genome-analysis/tree/HEAD/assembly/short-read-assembly) · ★1)
- **short-read-barcode-frequency-filtering**: Use when you have a 10x Genomics short-read BAM (from Cell Ranger mapping/filtering/UMI counting) and need a compact whitelist of the most frequent cellular barcodes that cover the bulk of short reads before barcode matching to long reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-barcode-frequency-filtering))
- **short-read-clustering-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating clustering accuracy (ACC) of a DNA sequence clustering tool on synthetic FASTA datasets, especially regimes excluded from the iDeLUCS paper: average read length &lt;500 bp or expected cluster counts above 200 (e.g. ~5000). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-clustering-benchmarking))
- **short-read-contamination-quantification**: Use when sequencing cDNA libraries of an in vitro-transcribed mRNA product (Illumina TruSeq short reads or ONT long reads) and you need to compute the fraction of reads attributable to the intended mRNA versus off-target RNAs and bacterial contamination. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-contamination-quantification))
- **short-read-coverage-subsampling**: Use when you must benchmark a retrieval/assembly pipeline across sequencing depths and only full-coverage paired-end Illumina reads (e.g. D. gyrociliatus PRJEB37657) are available — subsample to simulate lower-coverage genome-skimming arms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-coverage-subsampling))
- **short-read-de-novo-contig-assembly**: Use when ~50x Illumina FASTQ reads are available and accurate short contigs are needed as the contig input for a DBG2OLC hybrid assembly or similar long-read scaffolding pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-de-novo-contig-assembly))
- **short-read-de-novo-transcriptome-assembly**: Use when short-read FASTQ data must be assembled de novo and the resulting contigs need to be checked against a long-read-defined isoform set to assess whether complex/multi-isoform loci are recovered. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-de-novo-transcriptome-assembly))
- **short-read-directional-extension-to-hdf**: Use when ChIP-Seq short-read alignments (BED/ELAND) must be converted into a per-bin coverage profile that reflects fragment-length uncertainty, e.g. before HMM-based peak calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/short-read-directional-extension-to-hdf))
- **short-read-genome-assembly-spades**: Use when starting a hybrid assembly from Illumina MiSeq paired-end FASTQ (e.g. ERR654977, Kp2146_paired_*.fastq.gz) that will subsequently be completed with long nanopore reads via npScarf. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-genome-assembly-spades))
- **short-read-germline-calling**: Use when aligning short reads and calling, joint genotyping, filtering, or benchmarking germline SNVs and indels from WGS, WES, or panels. Not for SV or CNV (sv-cnv-calling), tumor samples (somatic-calling), or annotation. ([oumarkme/research_agents](https://github.com/oumarkme/research_agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/bioinfo-lab/skills/short-read-germline-calling))
- **short-read-graph-assembly**: Use when assembling de novo transcriptomes from Illumina RNA-Seq FASTQ reads and the goal is a condensed de Bruijn graph whose branching vertices preserve multiple paths through shared k-mers (alternative isoforms) rather than collapsing them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-graph-assembly))
- **short-read-indel-variant-calling**: Use when you have parental paired-end FASTQ reads (e.g. EcoRI RAD-seq) and a reference FASTA, and need a catalog of indel sites between two parents of a mapping population for downstream genotyping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-indel-variant-calling))
- **short-read-long-read-sv-concordance-analysis**: Use when you have matched long-read (MinION/nanopore) and short-read (Illumina) SV call sets from the same genomes and need to determine which SVs are long-read-specific and characterize their size, GC, and repeat content. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-long-read-sv-concordance-analysis))
- **short-read-mapping-to-allele-database**: Use when WGS read pairs must be aligned against known MLST allele sequences so per-read allele match/mismatch evidence (e.g., Phred-weighted mismatches) can be extracted as ILP input for strain deconvolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-mapping-to-allele-database))
- **short-read-mapping-to-genome-databases**: Use when paired-end shotgun metagenomic FASTQ reads from water samples must be attributed to competing fecal source categories (e.g., human, sewage, septage, cow, dog, goose) using a genome-to-source database without sequencing the sources themselves. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-mapping-to-genome-databases))
- **short-read-methylation-correlation-analysis**: Use when evaluating or comparing Nanopore 5mC methylation callers against short-read ground truth: you have per-read modBAM/TSV calls plus a short-read methylation reference (e.g. EpiQC, GSE186383) and need per-site F1 and frequency correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-methylation-correlation-analysis))
- **short-read-preprocessing**: Use when raw Illumina FASTQ reads have heterogeneous lengths or trailing low-quality bases that would distort k-mer spectra, and the downstream goal is k-mer histogram generation for genome size, repeat, and heterozygosity estimation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-preprocessing))
- **short-read-quality-filtering**: Use when preparing Illumina/short-read data as input to yak k-mer counting for NextPolish2 polishing or Merqury/yak QV evaluation, and reads need adapter removal, quality trimming, and filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-quality-filtering))
- **short-read-quality-filtering**: Use when raw paired-end short-read (Illumina) FASTQ files must be cleaned of low-quality reads, adapters and poor-quality trailing bases before hybrid assembly or downstream polishing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-quality-filtering))
- **short-read-quality-trimming**: Use when raw Illumina FASTQ and/or Nanopore long-read FASTQ (e.g. plasmid datasets from GigaDB) must be trimmed before assembly with Unicycler, Filtlong, or Bandage graph inspection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-quality-trimming))
- **short-read-quality-trimming**: Use when raw Illumina paired-end FASTQ reads must be quality-trimmed and adapter-stripped to remove low-quality sequence and adapters before targeted assembly or seed-read searching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-quality-trimming--5349220210118df8))
- **short-read-quality-trimming**: Use when raw Illumina paired-end metagenomic FASTQ/fastq.gz reads must be stripped of Illumina adapters and low-quality bases before downstream SSU rRNA filtering, assembly, or classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-quality-trimming--534731b241401dae))
- **short-read-quality-trimming**: Use when raw paired-end Illumina FASTQ (e.g. CAMISIM simulated metagenomic test data) enters nf-core/mag and must be adapter/quality trimmed and decontaminated before assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-quality-trimming--625771f623f96d83))
- **short-read-quality-trimming**: Use when raw paired-end shotgun virome FASTQ reads need adapter removal, quality trimming, and contaminant filtering as the first stage of ViroProfiler before assembly or viral identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-quality-trimming--d174ba798d451874))
- **short-read-reference-alignment**: Use when raw or adapter-trimmed FASTQ reads from ChIP-seq, RNA-seq, DNase-seq, MNase-seq, ATAC-seq, or GRO-seq datasets must be aligned to a reference genome to produce sorted BAMs with alignment statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/short-read-reference-alignment))
- **short-read-reference-alignment**: Use when raw tumor-normal WES/WGS FASTQ reads have been adapter-trimmed and must be aligned to a human reference genome to produce BAM files for downstream somatic SNV/INDEL calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/short-read-reference-alignment))
- **short-read-reference-mapping-with-bowtie2**: Use when you need to quantify how completely a raw or subsetted set of short paired-end reads (FASTQ) covers a specific reference contig (FASTA), e.g. tracking cas operon coverage across read-subset expansion cycles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-reference-mapping-with-bowtie2))
- **short-read-rnaseq-alignment-hisat2**: Use when short-read Illumina RNA-seq data (e.g. ENA PRJEB36584) must be aligned to a reference genome as part of a hybrid (PacBio + Illumina) genome-guided transcriptome assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-rnaseq-alignment-hisat2))
- **short-read-rnaseq-bsj-read-mapping**: Use when Illumina short-read RNA-seq (FASTQ) needs BSJ-spanning read counts per library to quantify circRNA back-splice junction proportions, e.g. as the comparator arm of an isoCirc enrichment analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-rnaseq-bsj-read-mapping))
- **short-read-simulation-and-mapping**: Use when building simulated benchmark read sets for the B. burgdorferi MLST deconvolution pipeline (ADP/SDP, EvoMod mechanisms, coverage/edit-distance sweeps) or when reads must be mapped to a known allele database to produce ILP input files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-simulation-and-mapping))
- **short-read-simulation-for-baseline-comparison**: Use when benchmarking an NRS/insertion detection method against baselines that require ordinary paired-end short reads, and ground-truth insertions (e.g. 2000 sequences of 50-6000 bp inserted into GRCh38) must be tested at matched coverage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-simulation-for-baseline-comparison))
- **short-read-simulation-from-reference-genomes**: Use when reads must be simulated from genomes whose true species are absent from every classifier database, in order to measure false-positive misclassification of taxonomic classifiers as a function of similarity to the closest database relative. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-simulation-from-reference-genomes))
